    generatedTracks: List<MusicTrack>,
    favoriteTracks: List<MusicTrack>,
    activeTrackId: String?,

    onPlay: (MusicTrack) -> Unit
) {

        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .windowInsetsPadding(WindowInsets.safeDrawing)

        contentPadding = PaddingValues(bottom = 112.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(18.dp)
    ) {
        item {
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))
            Text("Library", fontSize = 34.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Black, color = FlashPlayPalette.Ink)
        }

        item {
            TrackSection(
                title = "My Muse Works",
                tracks = generatedTracks,
                emptyState = "No generated music yet.",
                activeTrackId = activeTrackId,
                onOpen = onOpen,
                onPlay = onPlay
            )
        }

                title = "Favorites",
                tracks = favoriteTracks,
                emptyState = "No favorite tracks yet.",
                activeTrackId = activeTrackId,
                onOpen = onOpen,
                onPlay = onPlay
            )
        }
    }
}

private fun LibraryScreen(
    generatedTracks: List<MusicTrack>,
    favoriteTracks: List<MusicTrack>,
    activeTrackId: String?,
    onOpen: (MusicTrack) -> Unit,
    onPlay: (MusicTrack) -> Unit
) {
    LazyColumn(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .windowInsetsPadding(WindowInsets.safeDrawing)
            .padding(horizontal = 20.dp),
        contentPadding = PaddingValues(bottom = 112.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(18.dp)
    ) {
        item {
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))
            Text("Library", fontSize = 34.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Black, color = FlashPlayPalette.Ink)
        }

        item {
            TrackSection(
                title = "My Muse Works",
                tracks = generatedTracks,
                emptyState = "No generated music yet.",
                activeTrackId = activeTrackId,
                onOpen = onOpen,
                onPlay = onPlay
            )
        }

        item {
            TrackSection(
                title = "Favorites",
                tracks = favoriteTracks,
                emptyState = "No favorite tracks yet.",
                activeTrackId = activeTrackId,
                onOpen = onOpen,
                onPlay = onPlay
            )
        }
    }
}

[66] user: 1Feed 动画要和音乐状态绑定
Lottie/Canvas 动画不只是装饰，应该播放时更活跃、暂停时静止或变慢、切歌时有过渡。这样用户能直观看到“音乐活着”，app写代码让动画与音乐节奏呼应；2底部 Tab 命名可以更产品化agent修改为Mood；3生成失败恢复
失败后应该保留用户输入，并提供 Retry、Switch to instrumental、Random prompt 三个恢复动作。现在错误只是提示，行动不够明确。并且生成成功后给出弹出提示，用户点击跳转feed页面听音乐；4收藏之后缺少反馈

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const rootDir = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..");
const promptBankPath = path.join(rootDir, "android/app/src/main/assets/prompt_bank.json");
const outputDir = path.join(rootDir, "android/app/src/main/assets/seed_music");
const manifestPath = path.join(outputDir, "manifest.json");

const totalSongs = Number.parseInt(process.argv[2] || "20", 10);
const totalInstrumentals = Number.parseInt(process.argv[3] || "20", 10);
const endpoint = "https://api.minimax.io/v1/music_generation";
const model = "music-2.6";

function readProperties(filePath) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) return {};
  return Object.fromEntries(
    fs
      .readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")
      .split(/\r?\n/)
      .map((line) => line.trim())
      .filter((line) => line && !line.startsWith("#") && line.includes("="))
      .map((line) => {
        const index = line.indexOf("=");
        return [line.slice(0, index).trim(), line.slice(index + 1).trim()];
      }),
  );
}

function apiKey() {
  const envKey = process.env.MINIMAX_API_KEY?.trim();
  if (envKey) return envKey;
  const localEnv = readProperties(path.join(rootDir, ".env.local")).MINIMAX_API_KEY;
  if (localEnv) return localEnv;
  const secrets = readProperties(path.join(rootDir, "android/secrets.properties")).MINIMAX_API_KEY;
  if (secrets) return secrets;
  throw new Error("MINIMAX_API_KEY is missing.");
}

function slug(value) {
  return value
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
    .slice(0, 64);
}

function batchFileName(item, mode, index) {
  const prefix = mode === "Song" ? "song" : "instrumental";
  return `batch-${prefix}-${String(index).padStart(2, "0")}-${slug(item.title)}.mp3`;
}

function defaultLyrics(title) {
  return `[Verse]
I carry sparks through the quiet street
Every step finds a brighte<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="667" />ount}] retry ${attempt}/${attempts}: ${task.item.title}`);
      }
      return await generateOne(task.item, task.mode, task.index, task.count);
    } catch (error) {
      lastError = error;
      console.error(`[${task.mode} ${task.index}/${task.count}] attempt ${attempt} failed: ${error.message}`);
      if (attempt < attempts) {
        await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 12000 * attempt));
      }
    }
  }
  throw lastError;
}

async function main() {
  fs.mkdirSync(outputDir, { recursive: true });
  const bank = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(promptBankPath, "utf8"));
  const songs = bank.filter((item) => item.mode === "Song").slice(0, totalSongs);
  const instrumentals = bank.filter((item) => item.mode !== "Song").slice(0, totalInstrumentals);
  const queue = [
    ...songs.map((item, index) => ({ item, mode: "Song", index: index + 1, count: totalSongs })),
    ...instrumentals.map((item, index) => ({ item, mode: "Instrumental", index: index + 1, count: totalInstrumentals })),
  ];

  const manifest = readManifest();
  for (const task of queue) {
    const expectedFile = batchFileName(task.item, task.mode, task.index);
    const alreadyDone = manifest.find((entry) => path.basename(entry.file || "") === expectedFile);
    if (alreadyDone && fs.existsSync(path.join(outputDir, expectedFile))) {
      console.log(`[${task.mode} ${task.index}/${task.count}] skip existing: ${task.item.title}`);
      continue;
    }
    const entry = await generateWithRetry(task);
    manifest.push(entry);
    writeManifest(manifest);
  }

  const songCount = manifest.filter((entry) => entry.mode === "Song").length;
  const instrumentalCount = manifest.filter((entry) => entry.mode === "Instrumental").length;
  console.log(`complete: ${songCount} songs, ${instrumentalCount} instrumentals`);
  console.log(`app assets manifest: ${path.relative(rootDir, manifestPath)}`);
}

main().catch((error) => {
  console.error(error.message);
  process.exit(1);
});

import fs from "node:fs";

const rootDir = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..");
const outputDir = path.join(rootDir, "android/app/src/main/assets/seed_music");
const manifestPath = path.join(outputDir, "manifest.json");
const endpoint = "https://api.minimax.io/v1/music_generation";
const model = "music-2.6";
const targetCount = Number.parseInt(process.argv[2] || "50", 10);
const batchPrefix = "suno-instrumental";

const prompts = [
  ["Aurora Piano Drift", "cinematic neo-classical piano instrumental, felt piano, warm chamber strings, soft granular pads, emotional sunrise arc, delicate dynamics, premium streaming mix, no vocals"],
  ["Glass City Sprint", "future garage instrumental, shuffled drums, liquid sub bass, glassy synth plucks, rainy neon city atmosphere, elegant tension and release, no vocals"],
  ["Oceanic Deep House", "melodic deep house instrumental, ocean wave field recordings, rounded analog bass, airy chord stabs, soft four on the floor groove, uplifting sunset mood, no vocals"],
  ["Forest Lo-fi Lantern", "lo-fi chillhop instrumental, dusty vinyl texture, mellow Rhodes, nylon guitar phrases, soft brushed drums, peaceful forest night mood, no vocals"],
  ["Desert Post Rock", "cinematic post-rock instrumental, tremolo electric guitars, wide drums, desert wind ambience, slow build to bright emotional climax, no vocals"],
  ["Nordic Folk Pulse", "Nordic folk instrumental, nyckelharpa-like strings, frame drum, low drones, icy mountain atmosphere, ancient but modern cinematic mix, no vocals"],
  ["Tokyo Synthwave Rain", "synthwave instrumental, nostalgic analog leads, gated drums, warm arpeggios, rainy Tokyo night drive, polished retro-futuristic mix, no vocals"],
  ["Bossa Morning Light", "modern bossa nova instrumental, nylon guitar, upright bass, soft brushed percussion, gentle piano comping, cafe<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="2407" />ring(),
  };
}

async function generateWithRetry(item, index, attempts = 3) {
  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= attempts; attempt += 1) {
    try {
      console.log(`[${index}/${targetCount}] request${attempt > 1 ? ` retry ${attempt}/${attempts}` : ""}: ${item[0]}`);
      const entry = await generateOne(item, index);
      console.log(`[${index}/${targetCount}] done: ${entry.title} ${(entry.bytes / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)} MB`);
      return entry;
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`[${index}/${targetCount}] failed attempt ${attempt}: ${error.message}`);
      if (String(error.message).includes("daily usage limit reached")) throw error;
      if (attempt < attempts) await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 15000 * attempt));
    }
  }
  return null;
}

fs.mkdirSync(outputDir, { recursive: true });
const manifest = readManifest();
let completed = existingBatchCount(manifest);
let failed = 0;

for (const item of prompts.slice(0, targetCount)) {
  const index = prompts.indexOf(item) + 1;
  const fileName = `${batchPrefix}-${String(index).padStart(2, "0")}-${slug(item[0])}.mp3`;
  const exists = manifest.some((entry) => path.basename(entry.file || "") === fileName)
    && fs.existsSync(path.join(outputDir, fileName));
  if (exists) {
    console.log(`[${index}/${targetCount}] skip existing: ${item[0]}`);
    continue;
  }

  const entry = await generateWithRetry(item, index);
  if (entry) {
    const nextManifest = readManifest().filter((track) => path.basename(track.file || "") !== fileName).concat(entry);
    writeManifest(nextManifest);
    completed += 1;
  } else {
    failed += 1;
  }
}

const finalManifest = readManifest();
console.log(JSON.stringify({
  generatedBatch: existingBatchCount(finalManifest),
  totalManifest: finalManifest.length,
  instrumental: finalManifest.filter((entry) => entry.mode === "Instrumental").length,
  song: finalManifest.filter((entry) => entry.mode === "Song").length,
  failed,
}, null, 2));

import fs from "node:fs";
import http from "node:http";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const scriptDir = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const rootDir = path.resolve(scriptDir, "..");
const outputDir = path.join(rootDir, "local_generated_music/proxy");
const endpoint = process.env.MINIMAX_BASE_URL || "https://api.minimax.io/v1/music_generation";
const model = process.env.MINIMAX_MODEL || "music-2.6";
const port = Number.parseInt(process.env.MUSIC_PROXY_PORT || "18901", 10);
const host = process.env.MUSIC_PROXY_HOST || "0.0.0.0";
const publicBaseUrl = process.env.MUSIC_PROXY_PUBLIC_BASE_URL || `http://10.0.2.2:${port}`;

function readProperties(filePath) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) return {};
  return Object.fromEntries(
    fs
      .readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")
      .split(/\r?\n/)
      .map((line) => line.trim())
      .filter((line) => line && !line.startsWith("#") && line.includes("="))
      .map((line) => {
        const index = line.indexOf("=");
        return [line.slice(0, index).trim(), line.slice(index + 1).trim()];
      }),
  );
}

function apiKey() {
  const envKey = process.env.MINIMAX_API_KEY?.trim();
  if (envKey) return envKey;
  const localEnv = readProperties(path.join(rootDir, ".env.local")).MINIMAX_API_KEY;
  if (localEnv) return localEnv;
  const secrets = readProperties(path.join(rootDir, "android/secrets.properties")).MINIMAX_API_KEY;
  if (secrets) return secrets;
  throw new Error("MINIMAX_API_KEY is missing.");
}

function slug(value) {
  return String(value || "generated-track")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
    .slice(0, 64) || "generated-track";
}

function defaultLyrics(title) {
  return `[Verse]
I wake up with a little light
Carrying the dream through the morning sky
Every step becomes a melody<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="768" />le)}.mp3`;
  const filePath = path.join(outputDir, fileName);
  const bytes = Buffer.from(await audioResponse.arrayBuffer());
  fs.writeFileSync(filePath, bytes);

  return {
    title,
    prompt,
    mode: isInstrumental ? "Instrumental" : "Song",
    audio_url: `${publicBaseUrl}/files/${encodeURIComponent(fileName)}`,
    duration_ms: json.extra_info?.music_duration ?? null,
    model,
    bytes: bytes.length,
  };
}

function streamFile(request, response) {
  const fileName = decodeURIComponent(new URL(request.url, "http://localhost").pathname.replace("/files/", ""));
  const filePath = path.join(outputDir, path.basename(fileName));
  if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
    response.writeHead(404, { "Content-Type": "text/plain; charset=utf-8" });
    response.end("not found");
    return;
  }
  response.writeHead(200, {
    "Content-Type": "audio/mpeg",
    "Cache-Control": "no-store",
  });
  fs.createReadStream(filePath).pipe(response);
}

const server = http.createServer(async (request, response) => {
  try {
    const url = new URL(request.url, "http://localhost");
    if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/health") {
      sendJson(response, 200, { ok: true, model, outputDir });
      return;
    }
    if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname.startsWith("/files/")) {
      streamFile(request, response);
      return;
    }
    if (request.method === "POST" && url.pathname === "/generate") {
      const rawBody = await readRequestBody(request);
      const payload = rawBody ? JSON.parse(rawBody) : {};
      const result = await generateMusic(payload);
      sendJson(response, 200, result);
      return;
    }
    sendJson(response, 404, { error: "not found" });
  } catch (error) {
    sendJson(response, 502, { error: error.message || "music proxy failed" });
  }
});

server.listen(port, host, () => {
  console.log(`Music proxy ready on http://${host}:${port}`);
  console.log(`Android emulator base URL: ${publicBaseUrl}`);
});

+const batchPrefix = process.env.IOS_MUSIC_BATCH_PREFIX || "ios-random-instrumental";

+  const candidates = promptBank

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const rootDir = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..");
const promptBankPath = path.join(rootDir, "android/app/src/main/assets/prompt_bank.json");
const outputDir = path.join(rootDir, "android/app/src/main/assets/seed_music");
const manifestPath = path.join(outputDir, "manifest.json");

const totalSongs = Number.parseInt(process.argv[2] || "20", 10);
const totalInstrumentals = Number.parseInt(process.argv[3] || "20", 10);
const endpoint = "https://api.minimax.io/v1/music_generation";
const model = "music-2.6";

function readProperties(filePath) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) return {};
  return Object.fromEntries(
    fs
      .readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")
      .split(/\r?\n/)
      .map((line) => line.trim())
      .filter((line) => line && !line.startsWith("#") && line.includes("="))
      .map((line) => {
        const index = line.indexOf("=");
        return [line.slice(0, index).trim(), line.slice(index + 1).trim()];
      }),
  );
}

function apiKey() {
  const envKey = process.env.MINIMAX_API_KEY?.trim();
  if (envKey) return envKey;
  const localEnv = readProperties(path.join(rootDir, ".env.local")).MINIMAX_API_KEY;
  if (localEnv) return localEnv;
  const secrets = readProperties(path.join(rootDir, "android/secrets.properties")).MINIMAX_API_KEY;
  if (secrets) return secrets;
  throw new Error("MINIMAX_API_KEY is missing.");
}

function slug(value) {
  return value
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
    .slice(0, 64);
}

function batchFileName(item, mode, index) {
  const prefix = mode === "Song" ? "song" : "instrumental";
  return `batch-${prefix}-${String(index).padStart(2, "0")}-${slug(item.title)}.mp3`;
}

function defaultLyrics(title) {
  return `[Verse]
I carry sparks through the quiet street
Every step finds a brighte<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="667" />ount}] retry ${attempt}/${attempts}: ${task.item.title}`);
      }
      return await generateOne(task.item, task.mode, task.index, task.count);
    } catch (error) {
      lastError = error;
      console.error(`[${task.mode} ${task.index}/${task.count}] attempt ${attempt} failed: ${error.message}`);
      if (attempt < attempts) {
        await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 12000 * attempt));
      }
    }
  }
  throw lastError;
}

async function main() {
  fs.mkdirSync(outputDir, { recursive: true });
  const bank = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(promptBankPath, "utf8"));
  const songs = bank.filter((item) => item.mode === "Song").slice(0, totalSongs);
  const instrumentals = bank.filter((item) => item.mode !== "Song").slice(0, totalInstrumentals);
  const queue = [
    ...songs.map((item, index) => ({ item, mode: "Song", index: index + 1, count: totalSongs })),
    ...instrumentals.map((item, index) => ({ item, mode: "Instrumental", index: index + 1, count: totalInstrumentals })),
  ];

  const manifest = readManifest();
  for (const task of queue) {
    const expectedFile = batchFileName(task.item, task.mode, task.index);
    const alreadyDone = manifest.find((entry) => path.basename(entry.file || "") === expectedFile);
    if (alreadyDone && fs.existsSync(path.join(outputDir, expectedFile))) {
      console.log(`[${task.mode} ${task.index}/${task.count}] skip existing: ${task.item.title}`);
      continue;
    }
    const entry = await generateWithRetry(task);
    manifest.push(entry);
    writeManifest(manifest);
  }

  const songCount = manifest.filter((entry) => entry.mode === "Song").length;
  const instrumentalCount = manifest.filter((entry) => entry.mode === "Instrumental").length;
  console.log(`complete: ${songCount} songs, ${instrumentalCount} instrumentals`);
  console.log(`app assets manifest: ${path.relative(rootDir, manifestPath)}`);
}

main().catch((error) => {
  console.error(error.message);
  process.exit(1);
});

async function generateWithRetry(item, index, attempts = 3) {
  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= attempts; attempt += 1) {
    try {
      console.log(`[${index}/${targetCount}] request${attempt > 1 ? ` retry ${attempt}/${attempts}` : ""}: ${item[0]}`);
      const entry = await generateOne(item, index);
      console.log(`[${index}/${targetCount}] done: ${entry.title} ${(entry.bytes / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)} MB`);
      return entry;
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`[${index}/${targetCount}] failed attempt ${attempt}: ${error.message}`);
      if (String(error.message).includes("daily usage limit reached")) throw error;
      if (attempt < attempts) await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 15000 * attempt));
    }
  }
  return null;
}

fs.mkdirSync(outputDir, { recursive: true });
const manifest = readManifest();
let completed = existingBatchCount(manifest);
let failed = 0;

for (const item of prompts.slice(0, targetCount)) {
  const index = prompts.indexOf(item) + 1;
  const fileName = `${batchPrefix}-${String(index).padStart(2, "0")}-${slug(item[0])}.mp3`;
  const exists = manifest.some((entry) => path.basename(entry.file || "") === fileName)
    && fs.existsSync(path.join(outputDir, fileName));
  if (exists) {
    console.log(`[${index}/${targetCount}] skip existing: ${item[0]}`);
    continue;
  }

  const entry = await generateWithRetry(item, index);
  if (entry) {
    const nextManifest = readManifest().filter((track) => path.basename(track.file || "") !== fileName).concat(entry);
    writeManifest(nextManifest);
    completed += 1;
  } else {
    failed += 1;
  }
}

const finalManifest = readManifest();

  instrumental: finalManifest.filter((entry) => entry.mode === "Instrumental").length,
  song: finalManifest.filter((entry) => entry.mode === "Song").length,
  failed,
}, null, 2));

import fs from "node:fs";
import http from "node:http";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const scriptDir = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const rootDir = path.resolve(scriptDir, "..");
const outputDir = path.join(rootDir, "local_generated_music/proxy");
const endpoint = process.env.MINIMAX_BASE_URL || "https://api.minimax.io/v1/music_generation";
const model = process.env.MINIMAX_MODEL || "music-2.6";
const port = Number.parseInt(process.env.MUSIC_PROXY_PORT || "18901", 10);
const host = process.env.MUSIC_PROXY_HOST || "0.0.0.0";
const publicBaseUrl = process.env.MUSIC_PROXY_PUBLIC_BASE_URL || `http://10.0.2.2:${port}`;

function readProperties(filePath) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) return {};
  return Object.fromEntries(
    fs
      .readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")
      .split(/\r?\n/)
      .map((line) => line.trim())
      .filter((line) => line && !line.startsWith("#") && line.includes("="))
      .map((line) => {
        const index = line.indexOf("=");
        return [line.slice(0, index).trim(), line.slice(index + 1).trim()];
      }),
  );
}

function apiKey() {
  const envKey = process.env.MINIMAX_API_KEY?.trim();
  if (envKey) return envKey;
  const localEnv = readProperties(path.join(rootDir, ".env.local")).MINIMAX_API_KEY;
  if (localEnv) return localEnv;
  const secrets = readProperties(path.join(rootDir, "android/secrets.properties")).MINIMAX_API_KEY;
  if (secrets) return secrets;
  throw new Error("MINIMAX_API_KEY is missing.");
}

function slug(value) {
  return String(value || "generated-track")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
    .slice(0, 64) || "generated-track";
}

function defaultLyrics(title) {
  return `[Verse]
I wake up with a little light
Carrying the dream through the morning sky
Every step becomes a melody<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="768" />le)}.mp3`;
  const filePath = path.join(outputDir, fileName);
  const bytes = Buffer.from(await audioResponse.arrayBuffer());
  fs.writeFileSync(filePath, bytes);

  return {
    title,
    prompt,
    mode: isInstrumental ? "Instrumental" : "Song",
    audio_url: `${publicBaseUrl}/files/${encodeURIComponent(fileName)}`,
    duration_ms: json.extra_info?.music_duration ?? null,
    model,
    bytes: bytes.length,
  };
}

function streamFile(request, response) {
  const fileName = decodeURIComponent(new URL(request.url, "http://localhost").pathname.replace("/files/", ""));
  const filePath = path.join(outputDir, path.basename(fileName));
  if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
    response.writeHead(404, { "Content-Type": "text/plain; charset=utf-8" });
    response.end("not found");
    return;
  }
  response.writeHead(200, {
    "Content-Type": "audio/mpeg",
    "Cache-Control": "no-store",
  });
  fs.createReadStream(filePath).pipe(response);
}

const server = http.createServer(async (request, response) => {
  try {
    const url = new URL(request.url, "http://localhost");
    if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/health") {
      sendJson(response, 200, { ok: true, model, outputDir });
      return;
    }
    if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname.startsWith("/files/")) {
      streamFile(request, response);
      return;
    }
    if (request.method === "POST" && url.pathname === "/generate") {
      const rawBody = await readRequestBody(request);
      const payload = rawBody ? JSON.parse(rawBody) : {};
      const result = await generateMusic(payload);
      sendJson(response, 200, result);
      return;
    }
    sendJson(response, 404, { error: "not found" });
  } catch (error) {
    sendJson(response, 502, { error: error.message || "music proxy failed" });
  }
});

server.listen(port, host, () => {
  console.log(`Music proxy ready on http://${host}:${port}`);
  console.log(`Android emulator base URL: ${publicBaseUrl}`);
});
